- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/mutation/collision.py`**

```python
"""§8.3's collision policy. *"The engine should never silently overwrite an
existing file."*

The four behaviours are exactly the four user-approved ones the design lists. None
of them writes: each chooses an outcome and, where a file is to be written, a
destination path that has been checked free. The executor is the only writer, and
it never receives an occupied path -- which is what makes "no path overwrites" a
property of the code's shape and not of its care.

*"The collision rule must distinguish exact duplicates from different files that
happen to share a filename. A content-hash match supports deduplication review; a
filename match alone does not."* That sentence is why `MERGE_ONLY_IF_HASHES_IDENTICAL`
raises on a `name_only` collision instead of quietly falling back to a suffix: a
fallback would make a deduplication behaviour reachable for two different files.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sqlite3
import unicodedata
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from database_agent.events import append_event
from database_agent.identity import hash_file
from evidence_shape.canonical import canonical_json

from mutation.constraints import FilesystemConstraints
from mutation.names import collation_key, resolve_name
from mutation.plan import MovePlan
from mutation.vocabulary import (
    COLLISION_BEHAVIOURS, COLLISION_KINDS, COLLISION_OUTCOMES, CONTENT_HASH_MATCH,
    FILENAME_COLLISION_RESOLUTION, HALTED_AWAITING_USER, MERGED_NO_WRITE,
    MERGE_ONLY_IF_HASHES_IDENTICAL, NAME_ONLY,
    OLDER_SENT_TO_VERSION_FAMILY_REVIEW, PRESERVE_BOTH_DETERMINISTIC_SUFFIX,
    RETAIN_NEWER_OLDER_TO_VERSION_FAMILY_REVIEW, STOP_AND_ASK, SUBSYSTEM,
    SUFFIXED_PATH, check,
)


class MergeRefused(ValueError):
    """`merge_only_if_hashes_identical` selected for two different files."""


class SuffixExhausted(RuntimeError):
    """No free suffixed name inside the caller's attempt bound."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CollisionResolution:
    plan_id: str
    colliding_destination_path: str
    collision_kind: str
    incumbent_content_hash: str | None
    incoming_content_hash: str
    behaviour_applied: str
    outcome: str
    final_destination_path: str | None
    version_family_review_ref: str | None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        check(self.collision_kind, COLLISION_KINDS, name="collision_kind")
        check(self.behaviour_applied, COLLISION_BEHAVIOURS,
              name="collision behaviour")
        check(self.outcome, COLLISION_OUTCOMES, name="collision outcome")


def find_collision(directory: Path, safe_name: str, *,
                   constraints: FilesystemConstraints) -> Path | None:
    """The entry already in `directory` that `safe_name` would collide with.

    Compares `collation_key`s over the real listing rather than calling
    `Path.exists()`, because `exists()` answers the volume's question and not
    §8.3's: it misses an NFC/NFD pair on a case-sensitive volume, and it cannot
    tell a caller WHICH entry collided, which the collision record needs.

    `os.scandir` is used with `follow_symlinks` left alone: only names are read
    here, never targets, so a symlinked entry is reported as a collision by name
    and Task 9 decides what a symlink means.
    """
    if not directory.is_dir():
        return None
    wanted = collation_key(safe_name, constraints=constraints)
    with os.scandir(directory) as entries:
        for entry in entries:
            if collation_key(entry.name, constraints=constraints) == wanted:
                return directory / entry.name
    return None


def _free_suffixed_path(incumbent: Path, safe_name: str, *,
                        constraints: FilesystemConstraints,
                        suffix_for: Callable[[str, int], str],
                        max_suffix_attempts: int) -> Path:
    """The first suffixed name no entry in the directory collides with."""
    directory = incumbent.parent
    head, dot, tail = safe_name.rpartition(".")
    stem, extension = (head, f".{tail}") if dot and head else (safe_name, "")
    for attempt in range(1, max_suffix_attempts + 1):
        candidate = resolve_name(
            f"{suffix_for(stem, attempt)}{extension}", constraints=constraints,
            directory_byte_length=len(str(directory).encode("utf-8")),
            has_extension=bool(extension)).filesystem_safe_name
        if find_collision(directory, candidate, constraints=constraints) is None:
            return directory / candidate
    raise SuffixExhausted(
        f"no free name after {max_suffix_attempts} attempts; the product stops "
        "rather than choosing a name nobody can predict")


def resolve_collision(plan: MovePlan, *, incumbent: Path, incoming_path: Path,
                      incoming_hash: str, behaviour: str,
                      constraints: FilesystemConstraints,
                      suffix_for: Callable[[str, int], str],
                      max_suffix_attempts: int,
                      materialized: bool,
                      mint_id: Callable[[], str]) -> CollisionResolution:
    check(behaviour, COLLISION_BEHAVIOURS, name="collision behaviour")
    try:
        incumbent_hash: str | None = hash_file(incumbent, materialized=materialized)
    except OSError:
        incumbent_hash = None
    kind = (CONTENT_HASH_MATCH if incumbent_hash == incoming_hash
            else NAME_ONLY)

    final: str | None = None
    review_ref: str | None = None

    if behaviour == MERGE_ONLY_IF_HASHES_IDENTICAL:
        if kind != CONTENT_HASH_MATCH:
            raise MergeRefused(
                f"merge_only_if_hashes_identical may not be applied to a "
                f"{NAME_ONLY} collision: a filename match alone does not support "
                "deduplication (§8.3)")
        outcome = MERGED_NO_WRITE
    elif behaviour == PRESERVE_BOTH_DETERMINISTIC_SUFFIX:
        outcome = SUFFIXED_PATH
        final = str(_free_suffixed_path(
            incumbent, plan.filesystem_safe_name, constraints=constraints,
            suffix_for=suffix_for, max_suffix_attempts=max_suffix_attempts))
    elif behaviour == RETAIN_NEWER_OLDER_TO_VERSION_FAMILY_REVIEW:
        outcome = OLDER_SENT_TO_VERSION_FAMILY_REVIEW
        review_ref = mint_id()
        # Whichever file is OLDER goes to review; NEITHER is removed, because
        # §7.11 forbids deleting a user file and the incumbent is one. When the
        # incoming file is the newer one it is written beside the incumbent under
        # a deterministic suffix; when the incumbent is newer, nothing is written.
        if incoming_path.stat().st_mtime > incumbent.stat().st_mtime:
            final = str(_free_suffixed_path(
                incumbent, plan.filesystem_safe_name, constraints=constraints,
                suffix_for=suffix_for, max_suffix_attempts=max_suffix_attempts))
    else:
        assert behaviour == STOP_AND_ASK
        outcome = HALTED_AWAITING_USER

    return CollisionResolution(
        plan_id=plan.plan_id, colliding_destination_path=str(incumbent),
        collision_kind=kind, incumbent_content_hash=incumbent_hash,
        incoming_content_hash=incoming_hash, behaviour_applied=behaviour,
        outcome=outcome, final_destination_path=final,
        version_family_review_ref=review_ref)


def record_collision(conn: sqlite3.Connection, resolution: CollisionResolution, *,
                     file_id: str, created_at: str, component_version: str,
                     record_id: str) -> str:
    conn.execute(
        "INSERT INTO collision_resolutions (record_id, plan_id, "
        "colliding_destination_path, collision_kind, behaviour_applied, outcome, "
        "created_at, payload) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
        (record_id, resolution.plan_id, resolution.colliding_destination_path,
         resolution.collision_kind, resolution.behaviour_applied,
         resolution.outcome, created_at, canonical_json(asdict(resolution))))
    append_event(
        conn, event_type=FILENAME_COLLISION_RESOLUTION, file_id=file_id,
        content_hash=resolution.incoming_content_hash,
        old_path=resolution.colliding_destination_path,
        new_path=resolution.final_destination_path, subsystem=SUBSYSTEM,
        component_version=component_version, observed_at=created_at,
        explanation=canonical_json(asdict(resolution)))
    return record_id
```

### Task 7: Special objects, unavailability, and the cloud-sync pause

> **UNRESOLVED — F2. `package_bundle_unapproved` is a class name that contradicts its own rule.** Contract out §5 names the class as if an approval could lift it. SPEC §9's table says **"Refuse. Absolutely, with no override. … unlike every other row in this table, this one has no approved path (P3 SPEC, ratified 2026-08-20)."** This task keeps the SPEC's class **name** — a Contract out name is not a plan author's to change — and gives the code **no parameter through which an approval could arrive**, which is also how the live producer behaves: `scan_agent.exclusion.is_protected_container`'s own docstring says its `extra` predicate *"can only ADD: a caller cannot un-protect a `.app`, because the rule has no override and a predicate that could return False for one would be that override."* **The name is wrong and the behaviour is right. Flagged, not renamed.**

> **UNRESOLVED — F5. Locked files, files open in another application, aliases and shortcuts have no defined behaviour.** §8.3 requires one — *"The product needs defined behavior for locked files, files currently open in another application, permission failures, aliases, shortcuts, symbolic links, macOS packages, application bundles, network-mounted folders, removable storage, and cloud-synchronized directories"* — and supplies defaults only for symbolic links, package bundles, and unavailable source or destination. **This task implements only the defaults the design supplies.** A locked or open file is not detected and no refusal class exists for it; permission failure is covered by the fifth staleness trigger, which is why it is absent here too.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/mutation/special.py`
- Create: `tests/p12/test_p12_special.py`

**Interfaces:**

*Consumes:* `scan_agent.exclusion.{is_protected_container, PROTECTED_BUNDLE_SUFFIXES, REASON_PROTECTED_CONTAINER}`, `mutation.vocabulary.*`. **P12 imports P3's predicate and authors no bundle-suffix table of its own** — the table exists, it is ratified, and a second copy is the defect class this project has paid for most.

*Produces:*

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ObjectVerdict:
    refusal_class: str | None
    pause_reason: str | None
    detail: Mapping[str, object]

    @property
    def permits_mutation(self) -> bool: ...

def inspect_objects(*, source: Path,
                    destination_directory: Path,
                    source_root: Path,
                    destination_root: Path,
                    extra_protected: Callable[[Path], bool] | None,
                    conflict_copies: Callable[[Path], tuple[str, ...]],
                    dataless_of: Callable[[Path], bool]) -> ObjectVerdict: ...
```

**Done-means:** **10** in full and the decision half of **11** (the paused *result* lands on an execution record in Task 8).

> **The check order is the security rule, not a style choice.** `67` §0: protected material is *"MARKED AND COUNTED, NEVER OPENED"*. So the package/system-item test runs **first**, before any symlink resolution, any `stat` that could follow a link into a bundle, and any hash. It is answered from the **path shape alone** by P3's ratified predicate, whose unit of protection is the **subtree** — the docstring records that an earlier version tested only the entry's own suffix and *"protected `Numbers.app` and admitted `Numbers.app/Contents/sheet.numbers`"*. P12 also never receives such a file in the running system, because P3 creates no `files` row inside a protected container; this check exists so a future caller that constructs a plan by hand is refused too.

> **A dataless item is the existing "source unavailable" refusal, not a sixth trigger.** SPEC §9: *"Refuse — do not hash, copy, or download in order to move."* Materialization is a user action. `dataless_of` is injected because production answers it from `os.lstat(...).st_flags & SF_DATALESS`, which no fixture can produce portably, and a replay must be able to answer it from the bundle.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/mutation/special.py`**

```python
"""SPEC §9's settled defaults, and only those.

The order below is a security rule. `67` §0: protected material is MARKED AND
COUNTED, NEVER OPENED. So the package / system-item test runs FIRST, from the path
shape alone, before any symlink is resolved and before any `stat` that could follow
one into a bundle. The predicate is P3's ratified `is_protected_container`, whose
unit of protection is the SUBTREE; P12 authors no bundle-suffix table of its own
and imports P3's, because a second copy of a safety table is a second answer.

What is NOT here, deliberately: locked files, files open in another application,
aliases and shortcuts (F5 -- §8.3 demands a defined behaviour and supplies none),
and permission failure, which is §8.3's fifth staleness trigger and belongs to
`preconditions.py`.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from scan_agent.exclusion import REASON_PROTECTED_CONTAINER, is_protected_container

from mutation.vocabulary import (
    CLOUD_SYNC_CONFLICT, PACKAGE_BUNDLE_UNAPPROVED,
    SOURCE_OR_DESTINATION_UNAVAILABLE, SYMLINK_NOT_FOLLOWED, decline_message,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ObjectVerdict:
    refusal_class: str | None
    pause_reason: str | None
    detail: Mapping[str, object]

    @property
    def permits_mutation(self) -> bool:
        return self.refusal_class is None and self.pause_reason is None


def _refuse(refusal_class: str, **detail: object) -> ObjectVerdict:
    return ObjectVerdict(
        refusal_class=refusal_class, pause_reason=None,
        detail={**detail, "message": decline_message(refusal_class)})


def inspect_objects(*, source: Path,
                    destination_directory: Path,
                    source_root: Path,
                    destination_root: Path,
                    extra_protected: Callable[[Path], bool] | None,
                    conflict_copies: Callable[[Path], tuple[str, ...]],
                    dataless_of: Callable[[Path], bool]) -> ObjectVerdict:
    """One verdict for both ends of a move. No mutation, no read of any content.

    `extra_protected` is handed straight to P3's predicate, which can only ADD:
    a caller cannot un-protect a `.app`, because the rule has no override and a
    predicate that could return False for one would be that override.
    """
    for path in (source, destination_directory):
        if is_protected_container(path, extra=extra_protected):
            return _refuse(
                PACKAGE_BUNDLE_UNAPPROVED, path=str(path),
                rule=REASON_PROTECTED_CONTAINER,
                # Recorded so the refusal is legible without anyone reopening the
                # question of what is inside. The count is of this one item.
                marked_and_counted=1)

    if os.path.islink(source):
        return _refuse(SYMLINK_NOT_FOLLOWED, path=str(source),
                       target_not_resolved=True)

    for root, which in ((source_root, "source"), (destination_root, "destination")):
        if not root.exists():
            return _refuse(SOURCE_OR_DESTINATION_UNAVAILABLE,
                           unavailable=which, root=str(root))
    if not source.exists():
        return _refuse(SOURCE_OR_DESTINATION_UNAVAILABLE, unavailable="source",
                       path=str(source))

    if dataless_of(source):
        # SPEC §9: do not hash, copy, or download in order to move.
        # Materialization is a user action (`11-ops-runtime.md` §5).
        return _refuse(SOURCE_OR_DESTINATION_UNAVAILABLE, unavailable="source",
                       path=str(source), dataless=True)

    conflicts = tuple(conflict_copies(source))
    if conflicts:
        return ObjectVerdict(
            refusal_class=None, pause_reason=CLOUD_SYNC_CONFLICT,
            detail={"path": str(source), "conflict_copies": conflicts,
                    "message": decline_message(f"paused:{CLOUD_SYNC_CONFLICT}")})

    return ObjectVerdict(refusal_class=None, pause_reason=None,
                         detail={"path": str(source)})
```

### Task 8: The transaction — same-volume apply, V1–V3, directory creation, and the journal

> **UNRESOLVED — F13. §8.2 has no event type for a refused or paused action.** Done-means 13 requires that *"Every applied, refused, stale, paused, and undone action appended its §8.2 events"*, and the six types P12 authors contain no "refused move". This task appends **`failed move`** for a refused or paused attempt, carrying the exact result string in its structured explanation, because §8.2's list is P1's and minting a seventh name is a spec-level act with no run-time call (`database_agent/events.py`: *"Registration is a spec-level act (rule 4), so this table is compiled from the declaring SPECs and frozen at import"*). **A refusal is not a failure and calling it one is wrong.** The durable, correctly-named record is P12's own append-only `execution_records` row; the event is the §8.2 trace. Flagged, not resolved.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/mutation/context.py`
- Create: `src/mutation/journal.py`
- Create: `src/mutation/execute.py`
- Create: `tests/p12/test_p12_journal.py`
- Create: `tests/p12/test_p12_execute.py`

**Interfaces:**

*Consumes:* everything from Tasks 1–7, plus `database_agent.verify.{verify_content, VerificationPoint}`, `database_agent.files_table.observe_path`.

*Produces:*

```python

# src/mutation/execute.py
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ExecutionRecord:
    plan_id: str
    plan_version: str
    mode: str | None
    hash_at_preparation: str | None
    hash_immediately_before_move: str | None
    hash_after_completion: str | None
    destination_confirmed_pre_removal: bool | None
    result: str                            # applied | refused:<c> | stale:<t> | paused:<r> | failed:<c>
    final_destination_path: str | None
    directories_created: tuple[str, ...]
    started_at: str
    finished_at: str
    journal_entry_id: str | None

def apply_plan(conn, plan: MovePlan, *, context: ApplyContext) -> ExecutionRecord: ...
def execution_records_for(conn, plan_id: str) -> tuple[ExecutionRecord, ...]: ...
```

**Done-means:** the V1–V3 half of **4**, the creation half of **20**, the recorded-result halves of **10** and **11**, and the journal half of **13**.

> **The V3 ordering trap, and why `observe_path` is called before `verify_content`.** P1's `verify_content` hashes `files.current_path`, not a path handed to it. Called straight after a move, it would hash a path that no longer exists and return `"mismatch"` — reporting `failed` on every successful move. So the order is **move → `observe_path(destination)` → `verify_content(V3)`**, and `observe_path`'s own contract is the licence: *"Same bytes at a new path where the old path is gone: same file version, path updated, history retained (a move)."* This also satisfies the SPEC's *"path history (P1 retains it — P12 supplies old and new paths on the `executed move` event)"*.

> **Why `os.link` + `os.unlink` and not `Path.rename`.** On POSIX, `rename` silently replaces an existing destination — the one thing §8.3 forbids, and no amount of checking first closes the window between the check and the call. `os.link` fails with `FileExistsError` **atomically** if the destination exists, so the race has a correct answer instead of a lost file: a destination that appeared between the two checkpoints is `stale:destination_changed`, which is exactly what §8.3 calls it. Any other `OSError` from `link` on a same-volume pair means the filesystem does not support hard links; that propagates as an exception rather than becoming a result, because it is an environment fault and not something to tell a person about their file, and inventing a third failure class for it would be inventing vocabulary.

> **A V3 mismatch reports `failed` and does not roll back.** Done-means 4 requires `failed`, never `applied`. It does **not** authorize an automatic reversal: undo is conditional and per-entry (§8.3), and a rollback P12 performed on its own would be the all-or-nothing behaviour the batch contract rules out. The journal entry is still appended, with `post_move_verification_result` recording the mismatch, so the person can undo it deliberately.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing journal tests**

Create `tests/p12/test_p12_journal.py`:

```python
"""Contract out §6. The journal is append-only: an undo APPENDS a reversal entry."""
from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3

import pytest

from mutation import vocabulary as v
from mutation.journal import (
    JournalEntry, append_journal_entry, applied_entries, entries_for_plan,
    entry_by_id, is_reversed,
)


def _entry(entry_id="j-1", kind=v.ENTRY_APPLIED, reverses=None, plan_id="p-1"):
    return JournalEntry(
        entry_id=entry_id, entry_kind=kind, reverses_entry_id=reverses,
        plan_id=plan_id, organization_plan_version="plan-1", file_id="f-1",
        hash_algorithm="sha256",
        original_source_path="/fixture/Inbox/Syllabus.pdf",
        destination_path="/fixture/Coursework/PHYS1401/Syllabus.pdf",
        content_hash_at_movement="h1", collision_behaviour=None,
        post_move_verification_result="match", source_volume="vol-main",
        destination_volume="vol-main", execution_mode=v.ATOMIC_RENAME,
        directories_created=("/fixture/Coursework/PHYS1401",),
        intended_display_name="Syllabus.pdf", filesystem_safe_name="Syllabus.pdf",
        time_of_execution="2026-08-29T00:00:00Z", directory_reversal_outcomes=())


def test_an_entry_carries_8_3s_five_required_contents(p12_conn):
    entry = _entry()
    append_journal_entry(p12_conn, entry, record_id="rec-1")
    back = entry_by_id(p12_conn, "j-1")
    assert back.original_source_path == entry.original_source_path
    assert back.destination_path == entry.destination_path
    assert back.content_hash_at_movement == "h1"
    assert back.collision_behaviour is None
    assert back.post_move_verification_result == "match"
    assert back == entry


def test_an_undo_appends_a_reversal_and_never_edits_the_original(p12_conn):
    append_journal_entry(p12_conn, _entry(), record_id="rec-1")
    append_journal_entry(
        p12_conn, _entry(entry_id="j-2", kind=v.ENTRY_REVERSAL, reverses="j-1"),
        record_id="rec-2")
    assert entry_by_id(p12_conn, "j-1").entry_kind == v.ENTRY_APPLIED
    assert is_reversed(p12_conn, "j-1") is True
    assert len(entries_for_plan(p12_conn, "p-1")) == 2


def test_the_journal_refuses_a_delete_and_an_overwrite(p12_conn):
    append_journal_entry(p12_conn, _entry(), record_id="rec-1")
    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
        p12_conn.execute("DELETE FROM move_journal")
    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
        p12_conn.execute("UPDATE move_journal SET destination_path = ?", ("/x",))


def test_two_entries_cannot_share_an_id(p12_conn):
    append_journal_entry(p12_conn, _entry(), record_id="rec-1")
    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
        append_journal_entry(p12_conn, _entry(), record_id="rec-2")


def test_applied_entries_excludes_ones_already_reversed(p12_conn):
    append_journal_entry(p12_conn, _entry(), record_id="rec-1")
    append_journal_entry(p12_conn, _entry(entry_id="j-3", plan_id="p-3"),
                         record_id="rec-3")
    append_journal_entry(
        p12_conn, _entry(entry_id="j-2", kind=v.ENTRY_REVERSAL, reverses="j-1"),
        record_id="rec-2")
    assert {entry.entry_id for entry in applied_entries(p12_conn)} == {"j-3"}


def test_an_entry_kind_outside_the_vocabulary_is_refused():
    with pytest.raises(v.OutOfVocabulary):
        _entry(kind="rolled_back")


def test_a_reversal_must_name_what_it_reverses():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        _entry(entry_id="j-2", kind=v.ENTRY_REVERSAL, reverses=None)
```

```python
"""§8.3's transaction, on one volume. Plan -> recheck -> apply -> verify -> journal."""

from pathlib import Path

def _context(landscape, ids, *, gates=(), volume_of=None, conflicts=(),
             dataless=False, nodes=FIXTURE_NODES,
             legal=frozenset({"n-course", "n-phys"})):
    return ApplyContext(
        constraints=CONSTRAINTS, nodes=nodes, legal_destination_ids=legal,
        high_level_folders=landscape, gates=tuple(gates),
        suffix_for=lambda stem, attempt: f"{stem} ({attempt})",
        max_suffix_attempts=100, extra_protected=None,
        conflict_copies=lambda path: tuple(conflicts),
        dataless_of=lambda path: dataless,
        volume_of=volume_of or (lambda path: "vol-main"),
        now=lambda: "2026-08-29T00:20:00Z", mint_id=ids,
        component_version="p12-test", scan_state="included", materialized=True)

            extra_protected=None, conflict_copies=lambda p: (),

            dataless_of=lambda p: False, volume_of=lambda p: "v",
            now=lambda: "t", mint_id=ids, component_version="p12-test",
            scan_state="included", materialized=True)


def test_an_ordinary_move_applies_and_records_v1_v2_v3(p12_conn, planned,
                                                       landscape, ids):

    assert record.result == f"{v.PAUSED}:{v.AWAITING_COLLISION_DECISION}"

    assert record.result == v.APPLIED
    assert record.final_destination_path == str(
        destination.parent / "Syllabus (1).pdf")

def test_a_destination_taken_between_the_checkpoints_is_stale_not_overwritten(
        p12_conn, planned, landscape, ids, monkeypatch):
    """The `os.link` race, answered. Nothing is overwritten and nothing is lost."""
    plan, source = planned
    destination = Path(plan.resolved_destination_path)
    import mutation.execute as execute_module
    real_link = execute_module.os.link

    def racing_link(src, dst, **kwargs):
        Path(dst).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        Path(dst).write_bytes(b"someone else got there first")
        return real_link(src, dst, **kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(execute_module.os, "link", racing_link)
    record = apply_plan(p12_conn, plan, context=_context(landscape, ids))
    assert record.result == f"{v.STALE}:{v.DESTINATION_CHANGED}"
    assert destination.read_bytes() == b"someone else got there first"
    assert source.exists()


def test_a_v3_mismatch_reports_failed_and_never_applied(p12_conn, planned,
                                                        landscape, ids,
                                                        monkeypatch):
    plan, _ = planned
    import mutation.execute as execute_module
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        execute_module, "verify_content",
        lambda conn, file_id, expected, *, point, author, component_version,
        materialized: ("mismatch" if point.name == "V3" else "match"))
    record = apply_plan(p12_conn, plan, context=_context(landscape, ids))
    assert record.result == f"{v.FAILED}:{v.V3_HASH_MISMATCH}"
    assert record.hash_after_completion is None
    entry = entries_for_plan(p12_conn, plan.plan_id)[0]
    assert entry.post_move_verification_result == "mismatch"
    assert p12_conn.execute(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM events WHERE event_type = ?",
        (v.FAILED_MOVE,)).fetchone()[0] == 1


def test_every_result_lands_on_an_append_only_execution_record(p12_conn, planned,
                                                               landscape, ids):
    import sqlite3
    plan, _ = planned
    record = apply_plan(p12_conn, plan, context=_context(landscape, ids))
    assert execution_records_for(p12_conn, plan.plan_id) == (record,)
    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
        p12_conn.execute("UPDATE execution_records SET result = ?", ("applied",))


def dataclasses_replace_policy(plan, policy):
    import dataclasses
    return dataclasses.replace(plan, collision_policy=policy)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `src/mutation/context.py`**

```python
"""Every authority P12 needs and the design does not supply, in one frozen record.

`tree_design.config`'s rule holds here word for word: absent means refuse, never
guess. A default for any field below would be P12 deciding something the design
left to a platform table, to §1.1, or to the user -- which filesystem constraints
apply, what a deterministic suffix looks like, which folders are high-level, what
counts as a cloud conflict copy, or when a plan expires.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass, fields
from pathlib import Path

from tree_design.records import Node

from mutation.constraints import FilesystemConstraints
from mutation.plan import MovePlan


class ConfigurationRequired(RuntimeError):
    """An injected authority that is absent, or a bound that is not positive."""


_CALLABLES: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "suffix_for", "conflict_copies", "dataless_of", "volume_of", "now", "mint_id",
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ApplyContext:
    constraints: FilesystemConstraints
    nodes: Sequence[Node]
    legal_destination_ids: frozenset[str]
    high_level_folders: Mapping[str, Path]
    #: Evaluated in order at the pre-apply recheck. Each returns a refusal class
    #: or None. Task 10 supplies the two the SPEC names -- the review-approval
    #: gate and the protected-without-policy gate -- and the tuple is ordered so
    #: the one whose refusal an approval CANNOT lift is asked first.
    gates: tuple[Callable[[MovePlan], str | None], ...]
    suffix_for: Callable[[str, int], str]
    max_suffix_attempts: int
    extra_protected: Callable[[Path], bool] | None
    conflict_copies: Callable[[Path], tuple[str, ...]]
    dataless_of: Callable[[Path], bool]
    volume_of: Callable[[Path], str]
    now: Callable[[], str]
    mint_id: Callable[[], str]
    component_version: str
    scan_state: str
    materialized: bool

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.constraints, FilesystemConstraints):
            raise ConfigurationRequired(
                "constraints are the TARGET volume's rules and P12 authors none")
        for name in _CALLABLES:
            if not callable(getattr(self, name)):
                raise ConfigurationRequired(
                    f"{name} is an injected authority with no default; the design "
                    "states no answer for it and one chosen here would be P12 "
                    "authoring the design")
        if not all(callable(gate) for gate in self.gates):
            raise ConfigurationRequired("every gate is a callable")
        if (not isinstance(self.max_suffix_attempts, int)
                or isinstance(self.max_suffix_attempts, bool)
                or self.max_suffix_attempts <= 0):
            raise ConfigurationRequired(
                "max_suffix_attempts is a positive bound; an unbounded suffix "
                "search is a loop nobody chose")
        for name in ("component_version", "scan_state"):
            if not getattr(self, name):
                raise ConfigurationRequired(f"{name} is required and cannot be empty")
        if not isinstance(self.materialized, bool):
            raise ConfigurationRequired(
                "materialized is the caller's declaration that P3's dataless check "
                "has run; it is required, with no default")
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `src/mutation/journal.py`**

```python
"""Contract out §6 — the undo record. Append-only, forever.

*"An undo entry should include the original source path, destination path, content
hash at the time of movement, collision behavior, and post-move verification
result."* (§8.3). Those five are the first five fields below; the rest are what
REVERSAL needs and the SPEC adds.

An undo APPENDS a reversal entry; it never edits or removes the original (§8.2).
That is why this table has no `one_current` index: neither of the two entries
replaced the other, and a "current" row would claim one did.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass

from database_agent.files_table import HASH_ALGORITHM
from evidence_shape.canonical import canonical_json

from mutation.vocabulary import (
    COLLISION_BEHAVIOURS, DIRECTORY_REVERSAL_OUTCOMES, ENTRY_APPLIED,
    ENTRY_REVERSAL, EXECUTION_MODES, JOURNAL_ENTRY_KINDS, check,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class JournalEntry:
    entry_id: str
    entry_kind: str
    reverses_entry_id: str | None
    plan_id: str
    organization_plan_version: str
    file_id: str
    hash_algorithm: str
    original_source_path: str
    destination_path: str
    content_hash_at_movement: str
    collision_behaviour: str | None
    post_move_verification_result: str
    source_volume: str
    destination_volume: str
    execution_mode: str
    directories_created: tuple[str, ...]
    intended_display_name: str
    filesystem_safe_name: str
    time_of_execution: str
    directory_reversal_outcomes: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        check(self.entry_kind, JOURNAL_ENTRY_KINDS, name="entry_kind")
        check(self.execution_mode, EXECUTION_MODES, name="execution_mode")
        if self.collision_behaviour is not None:
            check(self.collision_behaviour, COLLISION_BEHAVIOURS,
                  name="collision behaviour")
        for _, outcome in self.directory_reversal_outcomes:
            check(outcome, DIRECTORY_REVERSAL_OUTCOMES,
                  name="directory reversal outcome")
        if self.entry_kind == ENTRY_REVERSAL and not self.reverses_entry_id:
            raise ValueError(
                "a reversal entry names the entry it reverses; an unattached "
                "reversal cannot be read as an undo of anything")
        if self.entry_kind == ENTRY_APPLIED and self.reverses_entry_id:
            raise ValueError("an applied entry reverses nothing")
        if self.hash_algorithm != HASH_ALGORITHM:
            raise ValueError(
                f"the journal records P1's algorithm, {HASH_ALGORITHM!r}")


def append_journal_entry(conn: sqlite3.Connection, entry: JournalEntry, *,
                         record_id: str) -> str:
    conn.execute(
        "INSERT INTO move_journal (record_id, entry_id, entry_kind, "
        "reverses_entry_id, plan_id, plan_version, file_id, original_source_path, "
        "destination_path, content_hash, time_of_execution, payload) "
        "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
        (record_id, entry.entry_id, entry.entry_kind, entry.reverses_entry_id,
         entry.plan_id, entry.organization_plan_version, entry.file_id,
         entry.original_source_path, entry.destination_path,
         entry.content_hash_at_movement, entry.time_of_execution,
         canonical_json(asdict(entry))))
    return entry.entry_id


def _from_payload(payload: str) -> JournalEntry:
    raw = json.loads(payload)
    raw["directories_created"] = tuple(raw["directories_created"])
    raw["directory_reversal_outcomes"] = tuple(
        tuple(pair) for pair in raw["directory_reversal_outcomes"])
    return JournalEntry(**raw)


def entry_by_id(conn: sqlite3.Connection, entry_id: str) -> JournalEntry | None:
    row = conn.execute(
        "SELECT payload FROM move_journal WHERE entry_id = ?", (entry_id,)
    ).fetchone()
    return None if row is None else _from_payload(row[0])


def entries_for_plan(conn: sqlite3.Connection,
                     plan_id: str) -> tuple[JournalEntry, ...]:
    return tuple(_from_payload(row[0]) for row in conn.execute(
        "SELECT payload FROM move_journal WHERE plan_id = ? ORDER BY record_id",
        (plan_id,)))


def is_reversed(conn: sqlite3.Connection, entry_id: str) -> bool:
    return conn.execute(
        "SELECT 1 FROM move_journal WHERE entry_kind = ? AND reverses_entry_id = ?",
        (ENTRY_REVERSAL, entry_id)).fetchone() is not None


def applied_entries(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> tuple[JournalEntry, ...]:
    """Every applied entry that no reversal entry has reversed.

    Task 12's directory reversal asks *"did another entry that is STILL APPLIED
    move a file into or beneath this directory?"*, and an entry whose own move has
    been undone must not hold a directory hostage.
    """
    return tuple(_from_payload(row[0]) for row in conn.execute(
        "SELECT payload FROM move_journal WHERE entry_kind = ? AND entry_id NOT IN "
        "(SELECT reverses_entry_id FROM move_journal WHERE entry_kind = ? "
        "AND reverses_entry_id IS NOT NULL) ORDER BY record_id",
        (ENTRY_APPLIED, ENTRY_REVERSAL)))
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement `src/mutation/execute.py`**

```python
"""§8.3's transaction, same-volume. The ONE place P12 writes to the filesystem.

*"The product must first create a plan, show it to the user where policy requires
review, validate that the plan is still current, apply one action at a time or in
a safely bounded batch, verify the resulting state, and record enough information
to undo the action later."*

The sequence below is that sentence, in order, with the two P1 traps handled:
`verify_content` hashes `files.current_path`, so V3 runs AFTER `observe_path`
moves the record; and it turns an `OSError` into `"mismatch"`, so the structural
checks run before it (`preconditions.py` states that half).

There is exactly one `os.unlink` of a user file in this package and it is not in
this module -- see `cross_volume.py`, where it is unreachable until V4 confirmed
the destination copy.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import sqlite3
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path

from database_agent.events import append_event
from database_agent.files_table import HASH_ALGORITHM, get_file, observe_path
from database_agent.verify import VerificationPoint, verify_content
from evidence_shape.canonical import canonical_json

from mutation.collision import (
    CollisionResolution, MergeRefused, find_collision, record_collision,
    resolve_collision,
)
from mutation.context import ApplyContext
from mutation.journal import JournalEntry, append_journal_entry
from mutation.plan import MovePlan
from mutation.preconditions import evaluate_preconditions
from mutation.special import inspect_objects
from mutation.vocabulary import (
    APPLIED, ATOMIC_RENAME, AWAITING_COLLISION_DECISION, DESTINATION_CHANGED,
    ENTRY_APPLIED, EXECUTED_MOVE, FAILED, FAILED_MOVE, PAUSED, PREPARE, PRE_APPLY,
    REFUSED, RESULT_KINDS, STALE, SUBSYSTEM, SUFFIXED_PATH, V3_HASH_MISMATCH,
    decline_message,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ExecutionRecord:
    plan_id: str
    plan_version: str
    mode: str | None
    hash_at_preparation: str | None
    hash_immediately_before_move: str | None
    hash_after_completion: str | None
    destination_confirmed_pre_removal: bool | None
    result: str
    final_destination_path: str | None
    directories_created: tuple[str, ...]
    started_at: str
    finished_at: str
    journal_entry_id: str | None
    collision_record_id: str | None
    detail: Mapping[str, object]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        head = self.result.split(":", 1)[0]
        if head not in RESULT_KINDS:
            raise ValueError(f"{self.result!r} does not start with one of "
                             f"{RESULT_KINDS}")


def _record(conn: sqlite3.Connection, record: ExecutionRecord, *,
            record_id: str) -> ExecutionRecord:
    conn.execute(
        "INSERT INTO execution_records (record_id, plan_id, plan_version, result, "
        "mode, final_destination_path, finished_at, payload) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
        (record_id, record.plan_id, record.plan_version, record.result,
         record.mode, record.final_destination_path, record.finished_at,
         canonical_json(asdict(record))))
    return record


def _not_applied(conn, plan: MovePlan, context: ApplyContext, *, result: str,
                 started_at: str, detail: Mapping[str, object],
                 **fields) -> ExecutionRecord:
    """Record a result that moved nothing, and append its §8.2 trace.

    F13: §8.2 offers no `refused move` or `paused move` type, so the trace is
    `failed move` carrying the exact result string. The correctly-named durable
    record is the append-only `execution_records` row above it.
    """
    finished_at = context.now()
    record = ExecutionRecord(
        plan_id=plan.plan_id, plan_version=plan.organization_plan_version,
        mode=None, hash_at_preparation=fields.get("hash_at_preparation"),
        hash_immediately_before_move=fields.get("hash_immediately_before_move"),
        hash_after_completion=None, destination_confirmed_pre_removal=None,
        result=result, final_destination_path=None,
        directories_created=(), started_at=started_at, finished_at=finished_at,
        journal_entry_id=None,
        collision_record_id=fields.get("collision_record_id"), detail=detail)
    append_event(
        conn, event_type=FAILED_MOVE, file_id=plan.file_id,
        content_hash=plan.expected_content_hash,
        old_path=plan.expected_source_path,
        new_path=plan.resolved_destination_path, subsystem=SUBSYSTEM,
        component_version=context.component_version, observed_at=finished_at,
        explanation=canonical_json({
            "plan_id": plan.plan_id, "result": result,
            "note": "not a failure -- §8.2 has no refused/paused type (F13)",
            **{key: value for key, value in detail.items()}}))
    return _record(conn, record, record_id=f"{plan.plan_id}:{finished_at}")


def _make_directories(directories: tuple[str, ...]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Create the ones that do not exist; report exactly those.

    Directory creation for a frozen node's path is itself a mutation and runs
    under the same transaction discipline (§8.3), since §5.1 and §5.12 leave
    frozen nodes as designed structure rather than real folders. Creating them
    shallowest-first means the returned tuple is already in the order undo must
    reverse (deepest-first) once it is read backwards.
    """
    created: list[str] = []
    for path in directories:
        directory = Path(path)
        if not directory.exists():
            directory.mkdir(parents=False, exist_ok=False)
            created.append(path)
    return tuple(created)


def apply_plan(conn: sqlite3.Connection, plan: MovePlan, *,
               context: ApplyContext) -> ExecutionRecord:
    started_at = context.now()
    source = Path(plan.expected_source_path)
    destination_directory = Path(plan.resolved_destination_path).parent

    # A source under no named §1.1 folder still has a real containing directory,
    # and its absence is exactly what "source unavailable" means.
    source_root = _anchor_of(plan.source_high_level_folder, context) or source.parent
    objects = inspect_objects(
        source=source, destination_directory=destination_directory,
        source_root=source_root,
        destination_root=context.high_level_folders[plan.destination_root_anchor],
        extra_protected=context.extra_protected,
        conflict_copies=context.conflict_copies, dataless_of=context.dataless_of)
    if objects.refusal_class is not None:
        return _not_applied(conn, plan, context,
                            result=f"{REFUSED}:{objects.refusal_class}",
                            started_at=started_at, detail=objects.detail)
    if objects.pause_reason is not None:
        return _not_applied(conn, plan, context,
                            result=f"{PAUSED}:{objects.pause_reason}",
                            started_at=started_at, detail=objects.detail)

    prepare = evaluate_preconditions(
        conn, plan, checkpoint=PREPARE,
        legal_destination_ids=context.legal_destination_ids,
        occupant_at_prepare=None, component_version=context.component_version,
        materialized=context.materialized, now=context.now)
    if not prepare.is_fresh:
        return _not_applied(
            conn, plan, context, result=prepare.verdict, started_at=started_at,
            detail={"trigger": prepare.trigger,
                    "message": decline_message(prepare.verdict)},
            hash_at_preparation=prepare.checkpoint_hash)

    for gate in context.gates:
        refusal = gate(plan)
        if refusal is not None:
            return _not_applied(
                conn, plan, context, result=f"{REFUSED}:{refusal}",
                started_at=started_at,
                detail={"gate": refusal, "message": decline_message(refusal)},
                hash_at_preparation=prepare.checkpoint_hash)

    pre_apply = evaluate_preconditions(
        conn, plan, checkpoint=PRE_APPLY,
        legal_destination_ids=context.legal_destination_ids,
        occupant_at_prepare=prepare.destination_occupant_hash,
        component_version=context.component_version,
        materialized=context.materialized, now=context.now)
    if not pre_apply.is_fresh:
        return _not_applied(
            conn, plan, context, result=pre_apply.verdict, started_at=started_at,
            detail={"trigger": pre_apply.trigger,
                    "message": decline_message(pre_apply.verdict)},
            hash_at_preparation=prepare.checkpoint_hash)

    target = Path(plan.resolved_destination_path)
    collision_record_id: str | None = None
    collision: CollisionResolution | None = None
    incumbent = find_collision(destination_directory, plan.filesystem_safe_name,
                               constraints=context.constraints)
    if incumbent is not None:
        try:
            collision = resolve_collision(
                plan, incumbent=incumbent, incoming_path=source,
                incoming_hash=plan.expected_content_hash,
                behaviour=plan.collision_policy, constraints=context.constraints,
                suffix_for=context.suffix_for,
                max_suffix_attempts=context.max_suffix_attempts,
                materialized=context.materialized, mint_id=context.mint_id)
        except MergeRefused as refused:
            return _not_applied(
                conn, plan, context,
                result=f"{PAUSED}:{AWAITING_COLLISION_DECISION}",
                started_at=started_at,
                detail={"collision": "merge refused on a name-only collision",
                        "reason": str(refused),
                        "message": decline_message(
                            f"{PAUSED}:{AWAITING_COLLISION_DECISION}")},
                hash_at_preparation=prepare.checkpoint_hash)
        collision_record_id = f"{plan.plan_id}:collision"
        record_collision(conn, collision, file_id=plan.file_id,
                         created_at=context.now(),
                         component_version=context.component_version,
                         record_id=collision_record_id)
        if collision.final_destination_path is None:
            return _not_applied(
                conn, plan, context,
                result=f"{PAUSED}:{AWAITING_COLLISION_DECISION}",
                started_at=started_at,
                detail={"collision_outcome": collision.outcome,
                        "behaviour": collision.behaviour_applied,
                        "message": decline_message(
                            f"{PAUSED}:{AWAITING_COLLISION_DECISION}")},
                hash_at_preparation=prepare.checkpoint_hash,
                collision_record_id=collision_record_id)
        target = Path(collision.final_destination_path)

    directories = _make_directories(
        tuple(str(part) for part in _designed_chain(destination_directory,
                                                    context)))

    # `os.link` fails with FileExistsError ATOMICALLY if the target exists, which
    # `rename` does not: on POSIX `rename` silently replaces. The race therefore
    # has a correct answer -- §8.3's `destination_changed` -- rather than a lost
    # file. Any other OSError here is a filesystem that cannot hard-link two paths
    # on one volume, which is an environment fault and not a user outcome.
    try:
        os.link(source, target)
    except FileExistsError:
        return _not_applied(
            conn, plan, context, result=f"{STALE}:{DESTINATION_CHANGED}",
            started_at=started_at,
            detail={"trigger": DESTINATION_CHANGED, "target": str(target),
                    "message": decline_message(f"{STALE}:{DESTINATION_CHANGED}"),
                    "detected": "at the link, between the recheck and the move"},
            hash_at_preparation=prepare.checkpoint_hash,
            hash_immediately_before_move=pre_apply.checkpoint_hash)
    os.unlink(source)

    stat_result = target.stat()
    observe_path(
        conn, target, author=SUBSYSTEM,
        component_version=context.component_version,
        filename=target.name, normalized_filename=target.name.casefold(),
        extension=target.suffix, observed_size=stat_result.st_size,
        observed_timestamps=str(stat_result.st_mtime),
        parent_folder_context=target.parent.name, mime_type=None,
        detected_format=None, scan_state=context.scan_state,
        materialized=context.materialized)
    v3 = verify_content(
        conn, plan.file_id, plan.expected_content_hash,
        point=VerificationPoint.V3, author=SUBSYSTEM,
        component_version=context.component_version,
        materialized=context.materialized)

    finished_at = context.now()
    entry_id = context.mint_id()
    append_journal_entry(conn, JournalEntry(
        entry_id=entry_id, entry_kind=ENTRY_APPLIED, reverses_entry_id=None,
        plan_id=plan.plan_id,
        organization_plan_version=plan.organization_plan_version,
        file_id=plan.file_id, hash_algorithm=HASH_ALGORITHM,
        original_source_path=plan.expected_source_path,
        destination_path=str(target),
        content_hash_at_movement=plan.expected_content_hash,
        collision_behaviour=(collision.behaviour_applied
                             if collision is not None else None),
        post_move_verification_result=v3,
        source_volume=plan.expected_source_volume,
        destination_volume=plan.expected_destination_volume,
        execution_mode=ATOMIC_RENAME, directories_created=directories,
        intended_display_name=plan.intended_display_name,
        filesystem_safe_name=plan.filesystem_safe_name,
        time_of_execution=finished_at, directory_reversal_outcomes=()),
        record_id=f"{entry_id}:0")

    applied = v3 == "match"
    result = APPLIED if applied else f"{FAILED}:{V3_HASH_MISMATCH}"
    append_event(
        conn, event_type=EXECUTED_MOVE if applied else FAILED_MOVE,
        file_id=plan.file_id, content_hash=plan.expected_content_hash,
        old_path=plan.expected_source_path, new_path=str(target),
        subsystem=SUBSYSTEM, component_version=context.component_version,
        observed_at=finished_at,
        explanation=canonical_json({
            "plan_id": plan.plan_id, "result": result, "mode": ATOMIC_RENAME,
            "journal_entry_id": entry_id,
            "directories_created": list(directories),
            "post_move_verification_result": v3,
            **({} if applied else {
                "message": decline_message(f"{FAILED}:{V3_HASH_MISMATCH}")})}))
    return _record(conn, ExecutionRecord(
        plan_id=plan.plan_id, plan_version=plan.organization_plan_version,
        mode=ATOMIC_RENAME, hash_at_preparation=prepare.checkpoint_hash,
        hash_immediately_before_move=pre_apply.checkpoint_hash,
        hash_after_completion=plan.expected_content_hash if applied else None,
        destination_confirmed_pre_removal=None, result=result,
        final_destination_path=str(target), directories_created=directories,
        started_at=started_at, finished_at=finished_at, journal_entry_id=entry_id,
        collision_record_id=collision_record_id,
        detail={"post_move_verification_result": v3}),
        record_id=f"{plan.plan_id}:{finished_at}")


def _anchor_of(root_anchor: str | None, context: ApplyContext) -> Path | None:
    """The §1.1 folder the source sits under, or None when it sits under none."""
    if root_anchor is None:
        return None
    return context.high_level_folders.get(root_anchor)


def _designed_chain(destination_directory: Path,
                    context: ApplyContext) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
    """The directories between the nearest existing ancestor and the destination.

    Read off the real filesystem rather than off the resolution record, because
    the resolution is a structural list and this is the moment the disk answers
    which of them exist. Shallowest-first.
    """
    chain: list[Path] = []
    current = destination_directory
    while not current.exists() and current != current.parent:
        chain.append(current)
        current = current.parent
    return tuple(reversed(chain))


def execution_records_for(conn: sqlite3.Connection,
                          plan_id: str) -> tuple[ExecutionRecord, ...]:
    return tuple(
        ExecutionRecord(**{
            **json.loads(row[0]),
            "directories_created": tuple(json.loads(row[0])["directories_created"]),
        })
        for row in conn.execute(
            "SELECT payload FROM execution_records WHERE plan_id = ? "
            "ORDER BY record_id", (plan_id,)))
```

### Task 9: Cross-volume copy-and-delete, and V4 before the source may be removed

> **UNRESOLVED — F7. What becomes of an unverified destination copy after a failed cross-volume move.** SPEC Open question 6, verbatim: *"§8.2 forbids removing the source until the destination hash is confirmed; §7.11 forbids deleting files. What becomes of the partial or hash-mismatched destination copy?"* This task **retains** it and records its path on the execution record and in the refusal detail, so the person can see both copies and decide. It is the only choice that cannot lose data, and it is **not** a resolution of the question — a product that leaves a half-copy on a drive after every failure has a different problem, and that trade-off is not P12's to settle.

> **UNRESOLVED — F14. Done-means 14's wording and the atomic-rename path.** Done-means 14 says *"the only source removal is the verified cross-volume case"*, but a same-volume move is `os.link` followed by `os.unlink` — the source path is removed there too. The two are not the same act: after a successful `os.link` the source and destination are **the same inode**, so unlinking one path removes a name and not a file. §7.11's actual sentence is *"must not delete files, mark them disposable, or move them out of a protected area without explicit user action"*, and neither branch does. **Task 14's guard therefore asserts the property §7.11 states — no call site removes a path unless the same bytes are already confirmed present at another path — rather than Done-means 14's literal sentence, which the atomic branch cannot satisfy and should not have to.**

**Files:**
- Create: `src/mutation/cross_volume.py`
- Modify: `src/mutation/execute.py`
- Create: `tests/p12/test_p12_cross_volume.py`

**Interfaces:**

*Consumes:* `database_agent.verify.{confirm_cross_volume_copy, verify_content, VerificationPoint}`, `mutation.context.ApplyContext`, `mutation.plan.MovePlan`, stdlib `shutil`, `os`.

*Produces:*

```python
class DestinationTaken(RuntimeError): ...       # O_EXCL lost the race

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CrossVolumeOutcome:
    confirmed: bool                     # V4
    copy_path: str
    source_removed: bool

def copy_exclusively(source: Path, target: Path) -> None: ...
def perform_cross_volume(conn, plan: MovePlan, *, source: Path, target: Path,
                         context: ApplyContext) -> CrossVolumeOutcome: ...
```

and in `execute.py`, one added dispatch:

```python
mode = (ATOMIC_RENAME
        if context.volume_of(source) == context.volume_of(destination_directory)
        else CROSS_VOLUME_COPY_AND_DELETE)
```

**Done-means:** the V4 half of **4**, **5** in full, and **14** as restated by F14.

> **Why `O_CREAT | O_EXCL` and not `shutil.copy2`.** `copy2` truncates an existing destination — the silent overwrite §8.3 forbids — and it does so without a check whose result could be trusted, because the check and the write are two calls. Opening with `O_EXCL` makes "create only if absent" one atomic operation, exactly as `os.link` does on the same-volume path, so both branches have the same guarantee and the same answer when they lose the race: `stale:destination_changed`. `shutil.copystat` runs afterwards so the copy keeps its modification time, which the collision policy's `retain_newer` comparison depends on.

> **The one authorized source removal in the whole package.** SPEC, Design slice owned: *"The single removal the design authorizes is removal of the **source** after a cross-volume copy whose destination hash has been confirmed."* `perform_cross_volume` reaches its `os.unlink` only inside `if confirmed:`, where `confirmed` is the return value of P1's `confirm_cross_volume_copy` and nothing else. There is no parameter, flag or branch that skips it.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing cross-volume tests**

Create `tests/p12/test_p12_cross_volume.py`:

```python
"""§8.2's V4. The destination copy is hashed and confirmed BEFORE the source may
be removed. A two-volume fixture is two directories plus an injected volume
oracle -- `volume_of` is injected precisely so a test can declare two paths to be
on different volumes without mounting one."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from mutation import vocabulary as v
from mutation.context import ApplyContext
from mutation.cross_volume import (
    CrossVolumeOutcome, DestinationTaken, copy_exclusively, perform_cross_volume,
)
from mutation.execute import apply_plan
from mutation.journal import entries_for_plan

from tests.p12.conftest import CONSTRAINTS, FIXTURE_NODES


def two_volumes(landscape):
    """`Documents` is one volume; anything under `Documents/Coursework` is another."""
    other = landscape["root_documents"] / "Coursework"

    def volume_of(path: Path) -> str:
        return "vol-external" if str(path).startswith(str(other)) else "vol-main"

    return volume_of


def _context(landscape, ids, *, volume_of, confirm=None):
    return ApplyContext(
        constraints=CONSTRAINTS, nodes=FIXTURE_NODES,
        legal_destination_ids=frozenset({"n-course", "n-phys"}),
        high_level_folders=landscape, gates=(),
        suffix_for=lambda stem, attempt: f"{stem} ({attempt})",
        max_suffix_attempts=100, extra_protected=None,
        conflict_copies=lambda path: (), dataless_of=lambda path: False,
        volume_of=volume_of, now=lambda: "2026-08-29T00:30:00Z", mint_id=ids,
        component_version="p12-test", scan_state="included", materialized=True)


def test_copy_exclusively_refuses_an_occupied_target(fixture_root):
    source = fixture_root / "a.txt"
    source.write_bytes(b"bytes")
    target = fixture_root / "b.txt"
    target.write_bytes(b"already here")
    with pytest.raises(DestinationTaken):
        copy_exclusively(source, target)
    assert target.read_bytes() == b"already here"


def test_copy_exclusively_preserves_the_modification_time(fixture_root):
    import os
    source = fixture_root / "a.txt"
    source.write_bytes(b"bytes")
    os.utime(source, (1_700_000_000, 1_700_000_000))
    target = fixture_root / "b.txt"
    copy_exclusively(source, target)
    assert int(target.stat().st_mtime) == 1_700_000_000
    assert target.read_bytes() == b"bytes"


def test_a_cross_volume_move_applies_and_records_all_four_points(
        p12_conn, planned, landscape, ids):
    plan, source = planned
    record = apply_plan(p12_conn, plan, context=_context(
        landscape, ids, volume_of=two_volumes(landscape)))
    assert record.result == v.APPLIED
    assert record.mode == v.CROSS_VOLUME_COPY_AND_DELETE
    assert record.destination_confirmed_pre_removal is True
    assert not source.exists()
    assert Path(plan.resolved_destination_path).read_bytes() == b"PHYS1401 syllabus"
    points = [json.loads(row[0])["point"] for row in p12_conn.execute(
        "SELECT explanation FROM events WHERE event_type = 'hashing' "
        "ORDER BY event_id")]
    assert points == ["V1", "V2", "V4", "V3"]


def test_a_same_volume_move_records_no_v4(p12_conn, planned, landscape, ids):
    plan, _ = planned
    record = apply_plan(p12_conn, plan, context=_context(
        landscape, ids, volume_of=lambda path: "vol-main"))
    assert record.mode == v.ATOMIC_RENAME
    assert record.destination_confirmed_pre_removal is None
    points = [json.loads(row[0])["point"] for row in p12_conn.execute(
        "SELECT explanation FROM events WHERE event_type = 'hashing' "
        "ORDER BY event_id")]
    assert "V4" not in points


def test_the_source_is_still_there_when_v4_could_not_confirm(
        p12_conn, planned, landscape, ids, monkeypatch):
    plan, source = planned
    import mutation.cross_volume as cross
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        cross, "confirm_cross_volume_copy",
        lambda conn, *, source, destination, expected_hash, author,
        component_version, materialized: False)
    record = apply_plan(p12_conn, plan, context=_context(
        landscape, ids, volume_of=two_volumes(landscape)))
    assert record.result == f"{v.FAILED}:{v.V4_DESTINATION_UNCONFIRMED}"
    assert record.destination_confirmed_pre_removal is False
    assert source.exists()
    assert source.read_bytes() == b"PHYS1401 syllabus"


def test_the_unverified_copy_is_retained_and_its_path_is_reported(
        p12_conn, planned, landscape, ids, monkeypatch):
    """F7. Retained, not deleted, and named so the person can see both."""
    plan, source = planned
    import mutation.cross_volume as cross
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        cross, "confirm_cross_volume_copy",
        lambda conn, *, source, destination, expected_hash, author,
        component_version, materialized: False)
    record = apply_plan(p12_conn, plan, context=_context(
        landscape, ids, volume_of=two_volumes(landscape)))
    unverified = Path(record.detail["unverified_destination_copy"])
    assert unverified.exists()
    assert unverified == Path(plan.resolved_destination_path)
    assert record.detail["message"] == v.decline_message(
        f"{v.FAILED}:{v.V4_DESTINATION_UNCONFIRMED}")


def test_a_failed_v4_appends_a_failed_move_event_and_no_journal_entry(
        p12_conn, planned, landscape, ids, monkeypatch):
    plan, _ = planned
    import mutation.cross_volume as cross
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        cross, "confirm_cross_volume_copy",
        lambda conn, *, source, destination, expected_hash, author,
        component_version, materialized: False)
    apply_plan(p12_conn, plan, context=_context(
        landscape, ids, volume_of=two_volumes(landscape)))
    assert p12_conn.execute(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM events WHERE event_type = ?",
        (v.FAILED_MOVE,)).fetchone()[0] == 1
    assert entries_for_plan(p12_conn, plan.plan_id) == ()


def test_a_successful_cross_volume_move_journals_its_mode_and_both_volumes(
        p12_conn, planned, landscape, ids):
    plan, _ = planned
    apply_plan(p12_conn, plan, context=_context(
        landscape, ids, volume_of=two_volumes(landscape)))
    entry = entries_for_plan(p12_conn, plan.plan_id)[0]
    assert entry.execution_mode == v.CROSS_VOLUME_COPY_AND_DELETE
    assert entry.source_volume == plan.expected_source_volume
    assert entry.destination_volume == plan.expected_destination_volume


def test_a_destination_taken_mid_copy_is_stale_not_overwritten(
        p12_conn, planned, landscape, ids, monkeypatch):
    plan, source = planned
    import mutation.cross_volume as cross
    real = cross.copy_exclusively

    def racing(src, dst):
        Path(dst).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        Path(dst).write_bytes(b"someone else got there first")
        return real(src, dst)

    monkeypatch.setattr(cross, "copy_exclusively", racing)
    record = apply_plan(p12_conn, plan, context=_context(
        landscape, ids, volume_of=two_volumes(landscape)))
    assert record.result == f"{v.STALE}:{v.DESTINATION_CHANGED}"
    assert Path(plan.resolved_destination_path).read_bytes() == \
        b"someone else got there first"
    assert source.exists()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/mutation/cross_volume.py`**

```python
"""§8.2's copy-and-delete, and V4.

*"If a cross-volume move uses copy-and-delete rather than an atomic rename, the
destination copy must be hashed and confirmed before the source can be removed.
This establishes file fixity: the system can show that a file at its destination
is byte-identical to the file it intended to move."*

The `os.unlink` below is the ONE removal of a user file the design authorizes, and
it sits inside `if outcome_confirmed:` where `outcome_confirmed` is the return
value of P1's `confirm_cross_volume_copy` and nothing else. There is no flag, no
parameter and no branch that reaches it otherwise.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import shutil
import sqlite3
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from database_agent.verify import confirm_cross_volume_copy

from mutation.context import ApplyContext
from mutation.plan import MovePlan
from mutation.vocabulary import SUBSYSTEM


class DestinationTaken(RuntimeError):
    """The exclusive create lost the race. §8.3 calls this destination_changed."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CrossVolumeOutcome:
    confirmed: bool
    copy_path: str
    source_removed: bool


def copy_exclusively(source: Path, target: Path) -> None:
    """Copy bytes into a target that must not already exist.

    `shutil.copy2` truncates an existing destination, which is the silent
    overwrite §8.3 forbids, and checking first does not help because the check and
    the write are two calls. `O_CREAT | O_EXCL` makes "create only if absent" one
    atomic operation -- the same guarantee `os.link` gives the same-volume path.

    `copystat` runs afterwards so the copy keeps its modification time, which the
    `retain_newer` collision comparison reads.
    """
    try:
        handle = os.open(target, os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
    except FileExistsError as exists:
        raise DestinationTaken(str(target)) from exists
    try:
        with os.fdopen(handle, "wb") as out, open(source, "rb") as incoming:
            shutil.copyfileobj(incoming, out)
    except BaseException:
        # The partial copy is P12's own, created microseconds ago, and removing it
        # here is not §7.11's case: no user file has been removed and the source
        # is untouched. This is the only unlink in the package that is not gated
        # on a confirmation, and it can only ever remove a file this function has
        # just created and never finished writing.
        os.unlink(target)
        raise
    shutil.copystat(source, target)


def perform_cross_volume(conn: sqlite3.Connection, plan: MovePlan, *,
                         source: Path, target: Path,
                         context: ApplyContext) -> CrossVolumeOutcome:
    """Copy, confirm at V4, and only then remove the source.

    A mismatch leaves the source exactly where it was and RETAINS the unverified
    copy (F7 -- SPEC Open question 6 is open, and this is the only choice that
    cannot lose data). The caller reports `failed:v4_destination_unconfirmed` and
    names both paths.
    """
    copy_exclusively(source, target)
    confirmed = confirm_cross_volume_copy(
        conn, source=source, destination=target,
        expected_hash=plan.expected_content_hash, author=SUBSYSTEM,
        component_version=context.component_version,
        materialized=context.materialized)
    if confirmed:
        os.unlink(source)
    return CrossVolumeOutcome(confirmed=confirmed, copy_path=str(target),
                              source_removed=confirmed)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Modify `src/mutation/execute.py` to dispatch on volume**

Replace the single `os.link` / `os.unlink` block with the two-branch form. Everything before it (objects, V1, gates, V2, collision, directories) and everything after it (`observe_path`, V3, journal, event, record) is unchanged.

```python
    mode = (ATOMIC_RENAME
            if context.volume_of(source) == context.volume_of(target.parent)
            else CROSS_VOLUME_COPY_AND_DELETE)
    confirmed: bool | None = None

    if mode == ATOMIC_RENAME:
        try:
            os.link(source, target)
        except FileExistsError:
            return _not_applied(
                conn, plan, context, result=f"{STALE}:{DESTINATION_CHANGED}",
                started_at=started_at,
                detail={"trigger": DESTINATION_CHANGED, "target": str(target),
                        "message": decline_message(
                            f"{STALE}:{DESTINATION_CHANGED}"),
                        "detected": "at the link, between the recheck and the move"},
                hash_at_preparation=prepare.checkpoint_hash,
                hash_immediately_before_move=pre_apply.checkpoint_hash)
        os.unlink(source)
    else:
        try:
            outcome = perform_cross_volume(
                conn, plan, source=source, target=target, context=context)
        except DestinationTaken:
            return _not_applied(
                conn, plan, context, result=f"{STALE}:{DESTINATION_CHANGED}",
                started_at=started_at,
                detail={"trigger": DESTINATION_CHANGED, "target": str(target),
                        "message": decline_message(
                            f"{STALE}:{DESTINATION_CHANGED}"),
                        "detected": "at the exclusive create, mid-copy"},
                hash_at_preparation=prepare.checkpoint_hash,
                hash_immediately_before_move=pre_apply.checkpoint_hash)
        confirmed = outcome.confirmed
        if not confirmed:
            return _not_applied(
                conn, plan, context,
                result=f"{FAILED}:{V4_DESTINATION_UNCONFIRMED}",
                started_at=started_at,
                detail={"unverified_destination_copy": outcome.copy_path,
                        "source_left_in_place": plan.expected_source_path,
                        "retained_because": "SPEC Open question 6 is open (F7)",
                        "message": decline_message(
                            f"{FAILED}:{V4_DESTINATION_UNCONFIRMED}")},
                hash_at_preparation=prepare.checkpoint_hash,
                hash_immediately_before_move=pre_apply.checkpoint_hash)
```

`ExecutionRecord`'s `destination_confirmed_pre_removal=confirmed` replaces the hard-coded `None`, `execution_mode=mode` replaces `ATOMIC_RENAME` in the journal entry, and `mode=mode` replaces it in the record. The added imports are:

```python
from mutation.cross_volume import DestinationTaken, perform_cross_volume
from mutation.vocabulary import CROSS_VOLUME_COPY_AND_DELETE, V4_DESTINATION_UNCONFIRMED
```

A `_not_applied` call now needs to carry `destination_confirmed_pre_removal`, so its signature gains that key through `**fields` and its `ExecutionRecord(...)` line reads
`destination_confirmed_pre_removal=fields.get("destination_confirmed_pre_removal")`; the failed-V4 branch passes `destination_confirmed_pre_removal=False`.

### Task 10: The review-approval gate and the protected-without-policy gate

> **UNRESOLVED — F12. `handling_class` reaches P12 twice, from two parts, and no rule says which wins on a disagreement.** P11's `PlacementDecision.privacy.handling_class` is carried on the decision and lands in the plan's `Sensitivity and consent state`; P7's `ClassificationStore.current(file_id, content_hash)` is the authoritative record under **D2**. They differ whenever the classification was superseded after the decision was made. This task reads **P7's live record** at the gate, because D2 makes it authoritative and because the gate runs at mutation time; the plan's carried copy stays exactly as recorded, as a statement of what was true when the plan was built. **Nothing here decides what should happen when they disagree** — the gate simply asks the authority — and a reviewer may reasonably want the disagreement itself to be a refusal.

> **UNRESOLVED — F15. §8.4's "user policy that explicitly permits it" has no producer.** §8.4 says protected material *"should not be moved automatically without a user policy that explicitly permits it"*, and nothing in `src/` is that policy. `movement_permitted_for` is therefore injected with **no default**, and the ordinary path — a callable that answers `False` — means **every protected file is refused**. That is the correct behaviour with no policy engine present and it must not be softened into a default of `True`. `66` §8's *"Exclusions"* row is where the policy will eventually live, and that layer is item 5 of `66` §22's release order.

> **`SENSITIVE_CLASSES` stays unpublished.** The gate reads `ClassificationRecord.protected` and nothing else. **C5** — *"is `protected` exactly the top two handling classes?"* — is open, and publishing the set here would answer it in code. The P7 Task 11 author removed exactly this set from their own draft for exactly this reason, and the restraint stands.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/mutation/approval.py`
- Create: `tests/p12/p13_fixtures.py`
- Create: `tests/p12/test_p12_approval.py`

**Interfaces:**

*Consumes:* `privacy.classification_store.{ClassificationStore, ClassificationRecord}`, `placement.vocabulary.{REVIEW_REQUIRED, BLOCKED_PENDING_USER, AUTO_ELIGIBLE}`, `mutation.plan.MovePlan`, `mutation.vocabulary.*`.

*Produces:*

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReviewApproval:
    approval_id: str
    plan_id: str
    placement_decision_ref: str
    plan_version: str
    required_review_policy: str
    verdict: str                       # one of REVIEW_VERDICTS
    presented_state_ref: str
    user_id: str
    decided_at: str

#: The two P11 policies that demand an approval before execution.
REVIEW_DEMANDING_POLICIES: tuple[str, ...]

def approval_satisfies(plan: MovePlan, approval: ReviewApproval | None) -> bool: ...
def refresh_requested(plan: MovePlan, *,
                      approval_for: Callable[[str], ReviewApproval | None]) -> bool: ...
def review_gate(*, approval_for: Callable[[str], ReviewApproval | None]
                ) -> Callable[[MovePlan], str | None]: ...
def protected_gate(*, classification_for: Callable[[MovePlan], ClassificationRecord | None],
                   movement_permitted_for: Callable[[MovePlan], bool]
                   ) -> Callable[[MovePlan], str | None]: ...
def classification_reader(conn) -> Callable[[MovePlan], ClassificationRecord | None]: ...
```

**Done-means:** **21** in full, including every one of its named negative tests.

> **Gate order: protected first, review second, and that is not cosmetic.** SPEC rule 3: *"An approval never overrides another refusal. It lifts `review_policy_unsatisfied` and nothing else."* Putting the protected gate first means a plan that is both protected-without-policy and unapproved is refused under `protected_without_policy` — the class that survives an approval — rather than under the one an approval would lift. Task 8's `context.gates` is an ordered tuple for this reason, and its `test_gates_are_evaluated_in_order_and_the_first_one_wins` is the pin.

> **P13 does not exist, and no source module pretends it does.** `approval_for` is an injected reader. `tests/p12/p13_fixtures.py` is the test-only producer and `src/mutation/` never imports it — the rule P9's plan set at the same seam (*"no source stub impersonates P13"*). When P13 ships, replacing the fixture with its published record is a one-line change at the injection point and a required integration test.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test-only P13 producer**

Create `tests/p12/p13_fixtures.py`:

```python
"""A test-only stand-in for P13's `review_approval` and its §8.2 event.

`src/mutation/` must never import this module. P13 owns rendering the plan,
collecting the gesture and appending `apply review approval` (SPEC, Contract in ->
From P13, rule 4); P12 only reads the record and checks four things about it.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3

from database_agent.events import append_event
from evidence_shape.canonical import canonical_json

from mutation.approval import ReviewApproval
from mutation.vocabulary import APPROVED

P13_SUBSYSTEM = "P13"


def approval_for_plan(plan, *, verdict: str = APPROVED, plan_version=None,
                      decision_ref=None, approval_id="ap-1") -> ReviewApproval:
    return ReviewApproval(
        approval_id=approval_id, plan_id=plan.plan_id,
        placement_decision_ref=decision_ref or plan.placement_decision_reference,
        plan_version=plan_version or plan.organization_plan_version,
        required_review_policy=plan.required_review_policy, verdict=verdict,
        presented_state_ref="presented-1", user_id="user-1",
        decided_at="2026-08-29T00:15:00Z")


def append_apply_review_approval(conn: sqlite3.Connection, approval: ReviewApproval,
                                 *, file_id: str) -> int:
    """P13's own §8.2 event, appended by P13. Already a registered type."""
    return append_event(
        conn, event_type="apply review approval", file_id=file_id,
        subsystem=P13_SUBSYSTEM, component_version="p13-fixture",
        user_id=approval.user_id, observed_at=approval.decided_at,
        explanation=canonical_json({
            "approval_id": approval.approval_id, "plan_id": approval.plan_id,
            "verdict": approval.verdict,
            "placement_decision_ref": approval.placement_decision_ref,
            "plan_version": approval.plan_version,
            "presented_state_ref": approval.presented_state_ref}))
```
